Immediacy marks completed readiness by eliminating hesitation between fitness and action.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Adjudication and Final Reckoning","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_13869938d9f751498a6c","text":"adjudication between people `ح ك م:B002/m01`, reckoning and judgment `د ي ن:B002/m01`, resurrection and standing `ق و م:B013/m01`, prior estimation `خ ل ق:B001/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Worship, Obedience, and Upright Disposition","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_16ea933acc72bd1a64d6","text":"worship and the worshipped one `ء ل ه:B001/m01`, obedience and religion `د ي ن:B001/m01`, inward disposition `خ ل ق:B004/m01`, moral straightness `ق و م:B008/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Combat and the Truth of a Charge","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_19bbe3744df92373bcf2","text":"Combatants resist one another with weapons, and a charge is judged by whether the attacker carries it through or falters.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Worn Cloth and Deceptive Ornament","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1a47df33cc340bb1a5dc","text":"Cloth loses pile and integrity through wear, yet coloring or ornament can make its visible state misrepresent its actual condition.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Edge, Enclosure, and Near-Far Relation","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1b961634a4d5202f621f","text":"95:2 `طور`; 95:3 `البلد`; 95:7 `بعد`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Udder Filling and Milk Cessation","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1c67402c0120ab68d205","text":"Udder filling establishes stored abundance, dependability frames the expectation of continued yield, and failed duration breaks that expectation. Cessation turns a bodily production scene into a concrete model of reliability and disappointment.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Lower Boundary and Outward Separation","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1ebb151e63d741dbe7f7","text":"Entities are located by edges, near and far relations, facing and trailing sides, and movement across a lower or outer boundary.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Delegated Judgment and Credited Testimony","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2013b795ca827c0ce001","text":"Trust grants standing to a witness or arbiter, delegated judgment gives that standing practical force, and attribution of truth or falsehood determines whether the entrusted word remains authoritative.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Run, Halt, and Backward Look","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_21efb2cf68b4d24c6456","text":"A wild animal runs a stretch, stops, and looks back before motion resumes or ends.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Debt, Dealing, and Rescission","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_233d1fe1b6d71a7a79ed","text":"95:5 `رددناه`; 95:6 `عملوا`، `الصالحات`; 95:7 `الدين`; 95:8 `أحكم`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Run, Halt, and Backward Look","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_26b0abd41271cec47ef2","text":"The run supplies forward motion, the stop breaks its continuity, and the returning gaze reverses orientation toward the path already crossed. Intermittence captures the repeated alternation between advance and pause.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Penetrating and Defective Judgment","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_278ca8ce9360cd24e1d7","text":"Penetrating counsel gives judgment depth, wisdom directs it toward the right result, and desirable goodness names its valued outcome. Weak opinion forms the reversal: a mind present to the matter but unable to penetrate it.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Worn Cloth and Deceptive Ornament","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_29158cc3a55f176a425d","text":"frayed and worn cloth `خ ل ق:B009/m01`, ornamented cloth that belies its state `ك ذ ب:B009/m01`, material frailty `م ن ن:B002/m02`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001046/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Office, Custom, and Civic Stewardship","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_29fc5cb5ca53d455883e","text":"public appointment `ع م ل:B003/m01`, governance and stewardship `ق و م:B004/m02`, habitual order `د ي ن:B005/m01`, settled residence `ب ل د:B009/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Debt, Dealing, and Rescission","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2c85040b7ed619ebdaf8","text":"Parties enter a deferred obligation, transact with one another, settle conflict, and may reclaim or unwind the exchange.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Worn Cloth and Deceptive Ornament","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2dfd4f322e185b487d19","text":"Wear reduces the material body of the cloth, frailty names its diminished resistance, and ornament supplies a contrary visual message. Appearance and integrity therefore move in opposite directions.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Suitability and Immediate Readiness","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2fc9a42788d017cf07a9","text":"predisposition and worthiness `خ ل ق:B005/m01`, functional suitability `ص ل ح:B003/m01`, utmost effort `ح س ن:B005/m01`, effortful exertion `ع م ل:B007/m01`, action without delay `ك ذ ب:B005/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Rank, Aptitude, and Counsel","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_324dfab3ab5499f418eb","text":"Human beings form provisioned groups structured by possession, labor, rank, kinship, protection, and intimate attachment.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Compatible Replacement and Restoration","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_333424146d8b88d002fd","text":"Substitution is not mere exchange: the replacement must fit the vacant function and be returned into the operative position. Immediacy closes the mechanism by preventing the restored function from remaining suspended.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Afterward, Interval, and Immediate Action","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_3591192e13d85d19925c","text":"`After` establishes ordinary succession, the intermittent construction inserts a gap before recurrence, and immediacy removes that gap. The three motifs define a compact scale from immediate action to delayed return.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Delegated Judgment and Credited Testimony","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_37412bc6cc31a0fbea42","text":"delegated arbitration `ح ك م:B005/m01`, crediting testimony or oath `د ي ن:B007/m01`, assent and belief `ء م ن:B002/m01`, attribution of falsehood `ك ذ ب:B002/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Combat and the Truth of a Charge","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_387da96ee61addf4e841","text":"Hostility carries opposition into the encounter, armed combat gives it an instrument, and resistance holds the opponents against one another. The charge then tests resolve in motion: its truth is enacted by completion and its falsehood by stopping short.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Uncut Continuity and Material Cessation","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_392736a3405ceaba9dce","text":"Processes unfold through ordered stages, return in intervals, or terminate when their duration, material, or force is cut.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Exact and Beneficial Finish","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_39ed1b659eab2c75be9f","text":"95:4 `أحسن`; The social act of discrediting the speaker completes the movement from manufactured claim to refused claim.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Inquiry, Urging, and Handover","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_4daaf149f2952a66f3a0","text":"Inquiry identifies the unknown item, the urging formula converts it into something incumbent, and handover presents it for uptake. The discourse moves from asking to obligating to giving.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Inquiry, Urging, and Handover","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_4e81ca5e68c5694e02eb","text":"A speaker asks what is at issue, urges an obligation, and places an offered object or command before the addressee.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"E:Bondage, Restitution, and Unransomed Release","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_50d935654b1233046a00","text":"Subjugation establishes control over the person, recovery reverses possession, and gratuitous release ends captivity without equivalent exchange. sequences.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Office, Custom, and Civic
